Training/data are in Chapters 23-24. Phase numbering, I0-I9, D0-D8, ownership, coding-agent ticket format, compute tiers, minimum serious demo and full target are in Chapters 25-26. The hypothesis registry, X-E and SYS-E experiments, kill rules, claim ladder and IP notebook are in Chapter 27. Contract ambiguities and acceptance-record requirements are in Chapter 28.
No tests, checkpoints, training runs, datasets or physical trials are claimed to have been executed by this specification. The acceptance criteria define required work. All proposed mechanism novelty remains a research hypothesis.
# 30. Execution Authority and Architecture Fidelity Ledger
## Purpose and precedence
This chapter corrects a dangerous reading of this specification: a complete system specification is not permission to silently invent a final neural architecture where Conrad’s source design did not choose one. It is a production authority for every item marked FROZEN or INTERFACE FROZEN, an experimental authority for every item marked EXPERIMENTAL, and a stop condition for every item marked OPEN.
The implementation authority order is:
A later explicit Conrad source decision, if it exists and is recorded in the source traceability table.
A FROZEN requirement, invariant, schema, API, state definition, loss target, test or acceptance criterion in this document.
An EXPERIMENTAL mechanism implemented as a named, configurable candidate under its registered experiment and comparator.
An OPEN item only after a recorded Architecture Decision Record (ADR), experiment result, or joint Burak-Kerem interface decision resolves it.
An implementation must never promote level 3 or 4 into level 1 or 2 merely because code has been written. The first implementation of an experimental module is a candidate, not “the Conrad model.”
## Required agent behavior
Kerem’s coding agent is authorized to implement the repository, schemas, APIs, deterministic test fixtures, Twin truth generators, data adapters, baseline models, experiment harnesses and pluggable candidate modules. It is not authorized to call an unselected backbone, message-passing network, latent size, optimizer, scheduler, loss weight, dataset, threshold, or control tolerance a frozen Conrad design.
Every implementation package must contain:
implementation_status: FROZEN_CONTRACT | EXPERIMENTAL_CANDIDATE | OPEN_BLOCKED
source_sections: [chapter/section IDs]
configuration_keys: [all variable choices]
assumptions: [each non-source choice]
baselines: [matched comparators]
acceptance_tests: [test IDs]
claim_status: NONE | IMPLEMENTED | EVALUATED | VALIDATED
OPEN_BLOCKED means the agent may create the interface and test fixture, but must not create a canonical learned implementation. EXPERIMENTAL_CANDIDATE means the agent may implement a candidate only behind a configuration key and only with its named baseline/ablation plan. FROZEN_CONTRACT means the behavior, data structure or safety invariant is mandatory and must be tested.
## Non-negotiable invariants
The following are frozen and every package must test them:
ID | Frozen invariant | Required test evidence
INV-ARCH-01 | Twins hold privileged truth only; Model 2 cannot consume twin truth at inference. | Static input audit and truth-leakage test.
INV-ARCH-02 | Model 2 holds belief and uncertainty; Model 1 does not create world facts. | Decision input/provenance test.
INV-ARCH-03 | Model 1 proposes within constrained action vocabulary; deterministic safety accepts or rejects execution. | Invalid-action and safety-override tests.
INV-ARCH-04 | Twin 1 is scrapped and cannot be reintroduced as an authority or dependency. | Dependency/architecture audit.
INV-ARCH-05 | Evidence, belief revisions, uncertainty, IDs, timestamps, frames and provenance remain distinct and traceable. | Round-trip, duplicate-evidence, late-message and provenance-DAG tests.
INV-ARCH-06 | Observed, inferred, predicted and unknown status are not interchangeable. | Spatial and domain status tests.
INV-ARCH-07 | A simulator hidden entity ID is never an inference feature or a production belief ID. | Feature-schema audit.
INV-ARCH-08 | A physical parameter unavailable from Burak’s characterization record remains OPEN; nominal values are labelled references. | RobotConfig provenance validation.
INV-ARCH-09 | Training/test/OOD lineage and hidden-state boundaries are immutable. | Split, lineage and label-access tests.
INV-ARCH-10 | No experimental gain becomes a product or research claim without registered evidence. | Claim registry review.
## Model and twin fidelity register
This table is the authoritative answer to “what can be built now?” It covers the entire V2 family. Chapter references contain the detailed tensors, equations, APIs, losses, baselines and experiments; this register governs the status of choices that cannot be truthfully inferred from a module name.
Package | Build now under this document | Frozen behavior and interface | Must remain configurable or experimental | OPEN blocker before a canonical final model | Required evidence to resolve
ECMER | Modality adapters, evidence envelopes, quality/missingness metadata, projection boundary, fusion test harness. | Evidence is typed, timestamped, framed, quality-aware and provenance-linked; no sensor is required to share an encoder. | Per-modality backbone, fusion operator, quality head form, embedding size. | Exact encoder/fusion family and dimensions. | Matched modality/fusion ablations, calibration and held-out missing-modality/OOD tests.
Association | Candidate generation, gating, NO_MATCH, identity lifecycle and truth-leakage tests. | Association uses allowed geometry/time/semantic/evidence context and supports no match. | Scoring network, thresholds, differentiable matching method. | Final scoring model and operational thresholds. | Identity-switch, false-merge, false-split and delayed-observation evaluation.
PBA and Persistent Belief Graph | Belief cell schema, revision graph, typed edges, query API, storage abstraction and replay. | Persistent IDs/revisions, property-level knowledge status, provenance and uncertainty are preserved. | Storage backend, retrieval index, graph batching and aggregation implementation. | Database and scalable graph execution choice. | Replay equivalence, bounded-memory, stale-update and graph-consistency tests.
BUO | Prediction/correction call boundary, direct-evidence update, conflict record and uncertainty outputs. | No duplicate evidence as corroboration; contradictions are recorded rather than averaged away. | Innovation/trust function, uncertainty updater, robust update family and thresholds. | Chosen Bayesian/evidential/ensemble/hybrid form and calibration mechanism. | Calibration, contradiction, repeated-evidence and shifted-noise experiments.
RBP | Typed relation schema, provenance-carrying relational update interface and contamination controls. | Relational outputs are inferred, never presented as direct observation; edge type and lineage are retained. | GNN/message function, propagation depth, aggregation and stopping policy. | Final message-passing architecture. | Edge-drop, wrong-edge, misleading-neighbor and hidden-region baselines/ablations.
TBD | Physical-time prediction API, delta-time handling, prediction status and correction merge. | Prediction is separated from evidence correction and carries prediction provenance. | GRU/LSTM, Transformer, SSM, neural ODE or engineering-plus-residual candidate. | Final temporal family and temporal latent structure. | Irregular-time, sparse-observation, long-horizon and OOD-dynamics comparisons.
PMBL | Create/retrieve/merge/split/dormant/archive lifecycle APIs and lineage retention. | Beliefs are persistent and merge/split does not erase history. | Learned retrieval, memory compression, policy/thresholds and backend. | Retention policy and production persistence implementation. | Long-sequence identity, recovery, memory-bound and audit-trace tests.
Model 2T | Structural graph, condition heads, factorized state contract, corrosion/fatigue V1 and TCDP boundary. | Structural topology, direct versus propagated evidence distinction, interpretable condition outputs and provenance. | TCDP message model, latent factorization size, residual dynamics and head architectures. | Final 2T neural blocks and qualified structural data manifest. | 2T-E01 through 2T-E10, direct-inference and propagation ablations, audited real-data transfer.
Twin2T and MCDE | Truth structural graph, degradation mechanisms, observation generator and train/test splitter. | Twin truth is separate, reproducible, mechanism-labelled and cannot leak to Model 2. | Distributional priors and residual stochasticity calibrated against evidence. | Valid parameter/data priors for target materials/sites. | Mechanistic sanity checks, held-out mechanisms and published/physical comparison where available.
Model 2E | Heterogeneous entity/field state, multi-scale/time interfaces, CEFD boundary and cross-domain message contract. | Entities and fields remain different state types; unsupported ecological causality is prohibited. | Field representation, entity encoder, heterogeneous update network, coupling operator and temporal architecture. | Final ecological/field neural design and vetted paired data. | Entity-only/field-only/uncoupled baselines, confounding checks and 2E-E01 through 2E-E12.
Twin2E and MEIFE | Habitat, field, entity and interaction truth layers; controlled observation/missingness generation. | Truth layers are explicit and their causal assumptions are logged. | Field solver/surrogate, stochastic population dynamics and parameter priors. | Valid ecological parameterization for each target environment. | Conservation/boundary sanity tests, ablations and data-grounded calibration.
Model 2S | UAHSM hierarchy, observed/inferred/unknown status, spatial belief API, counterfactual query and adaptive memory boundary. | Truth-observability-belief separation, no unjustified free-space claims, uncertainty/provenance. | Map representation, learned update/retrieval, hierarchical aggregation, occupancy/implicit/sparse model. | Final spatial representation and map-update backbone. | 2S-E01 through 2S-E09, occlusion, coverage, loop/revisit and counterfactual tests.
Twin2S and OCPWE | Canonical geometry, visibility/occlusion oracle, trajectory/observation generator and counterfactual world controls. | Truth is hidden from Model 2; observability is independently controlled; seed/replay determinism. | Procedural asset family, domain-randomization distributions and rendering/sonar realism. | Target-domain asset and sensor-prior validation. | Visibility oracle tests, same-world/different-view counterfactuals and transfer checks.
Model 1 and EGDC | Decision Claim Graph, claim/evidence query, constrained action vocabulary, deterministic constraint boundary, UIR instrumentation. | Decisions are grounded to beliefs/provenance; hard safety is external/deterministic. | Reasoning model, ranking/value model, claim aggregation and training objective. | Exact deliberative neural/structured reasoning architecture. | H-M1-01, X-E01/02, unsupported-claim, stale-belief, contradiction and OOD evaluations.
MCBR | InformationNeed, BeliefResolutionTarget, candidate observation schema, observability/cost interface and oracle evaluator. | It targets a specific knowledge gap and cannot command thrusters directly. | Information-value estimator, hypothesis representation, analytical versus learned ranking. | Final information-gain/ranking model. | Oracle regret, hypothesis-discrimination, redundancy, modality and mission-value experiments.
BAAC | Receiver belief model, semantic delta schema, progressive-fidelity envelope, channel simulator, queue/reconciliation interface. | Receiver knowledge, evidence availability and auditability constrain communication; raw evidence remains discoverable. | Scheduler, learned value, codec/fidelity predictor and channel predictor. | Final scheduler and codec policy. | FIFO/raw/fixed-priority/value-per-bit comparisons across outage/bandwidth conditions.
Navigation and state estimation | State/goal contracts, classical estimator/planner/controller baselines, allocator and safety APIs. | Model 1 decides what; MCBR chooses observation objective; navigation moves; control actuates; safety overrides. | Estimator family, planner, learned residuals and control policy selection. | Exact estimator/planner/controller after RobotConfig characterization. | Navigation, fault, latency, energy and hardware-in-the-loop acceptance evidence.
Unity V2 and RHI | 6-DOF interfaces, sensor/fault/power models, Python bridge, replay and Unity/physical adapters. | Same RHI semantics above adapter layer; all measurements and commands are timestamped/framed/provenanced. | Hydrodynamic coefficients, sensor distributions and fault parameters. | Measured RobotConfig, calibration and characterization data. | Unit, replay, physics sanity, parameter-ID and HIL evidence.
## Architecture decision record protocol
Every OPEN blocker in the fidelity register requires an ADR before it becomes canonical. A code comment, experiment config or informal chat message is insufficient. The ADR location is docs/adr/ADR-<id>-<slug>.md and must contain:
Title and date
Affected package(s) and source sections
Decision status: PROPOSED | EXPERIMENTAL | ACCEPTED | REJECTED | SUPERSEDED
Problem and options compared
Chosen option and exact configuration
Evidence links: run IDs, immutable config hashes, data manifests, seeds, metrics and plots
Comparator and ablation results
Failure modes and safety/epistemic impact
Compatibility/migration plan
Approval: Kerem; Burak only where a hardware interface or physical envelope changes
No ADR may cite a test-set result selected during tuning. No ADR may call a simulation-only result physical validation. No ADR may change the four-plane architecture or add a major subsystem without an explicit project-level architecture decision.
## Required repository closure map
The software repository is complete enough for a first serious V2 demonstration only when these paths exist, have tests and refer to the documented contracts. Names may vary only if a mapping is recorded in docs/IMPLEMENTATION_MAP.md.
conrad/
  schemas/                 # scenario, observation, evidence, belief, uncertainty, provenance, IDs/time/frames
  core/                    # ecmer, association, buo, rbp, tbd, pmbl, belief_graph
  domains/
    structural/            # model2t, tcdp, heads, adapters
    ecological/            # model2e, cefd, entity_field
    spatial/               # model2s, uahsm, map_memory
  twins/
    twin2t/                # mcde and structural truth
    twin2e/                # meife and ecological/environment truth
    twin2s/                # ocpwe and spatial/observability truth
  decision/                # egdc, claim_graph, constraints, outcome_monitor
  active/                  # mcbr, observability, candidate_generation, oracle
  communication/           # baac, receiver_model, channel_sim, reconciliation
  robotics/                # state_estimation, planning, trajectory, control, allocation, safety
  sim/                     # Unity bridge, robot config adapters, scenario execution, replay
  data/                    # manifests, adapters, licensing and split/lineage validation
  training/                # configs, trainers, losses, checkpoints and reproducibility
  evaluation/              # baselines, metrics, ablations, calibration, experiment registry
  tests/                   # contract, unit, integration, leakage, replay and acceptance tests
configs/                   # versioned, immutable run configurations
docs/adr/                  # architecture decision records
docs/IMPLEMENTATION_MAP.md # source chapter -> implementation path -> test IDs -> run IDs
The map must cover every row of the fidelity register. A missing path is an implementation gap. A path with untracked choices is an architecture-fidelity gap. A completed test without the required truth-leakage, provenance or baseline evidence is not a research-validation result.
## Completion claims
The following statements are allowed only at the stated maturity:
Statement | Minimum required evidence
“Contracts implemented” | I0 plus all relevant INV-ARCH and schema tests.
“Candidate module implemented” | Configured experimental package, unit tests and recorded assumptions.
“Mechanism evaluated” | Registered experiment, matched baselines/ablations, immutable splits and stored results.
“Mechanism retained” | Predeclared pass criteria met across appropriate in-domain, OOD and calibration evidence.
“Twin useful” | Matched twin-assisted versus real-only evaluation with no privileged truth leakage.
“System works in simulation” | Relevant I-gate passes using production contracts and replayable evidence.
“System works on robot” | Characterization, HIL and controlled physical acceptance evidence at I8/I9.
“Final Conrad architecture” | Every relevant OPEN row resolved by ADR and experimental evidence; all frozen invariants and target gates satisfied.
Until the final row is achieved, the correct description is: Conrad V2 concept and contract specification with configurable research implementations. This is not a weakness to hide; it is the only technically honest status until the experiments select the architectures.
# 31. Recovered Source Corpus Accounting
## Audit purpose
This register accounts for every recovered message in source_recovery/messages.json. It prevents a source predecessor from being silently lost simply because a later, more detailed design message is the chapter citation. DIRECT messages are cited as the primary authority for a chapter. INCORPORATED messages supplied concepts, constraints or earlier detailed treatment that were preserved and sharpened in the cited later chapters. SUPERSEDED messages used legacy naming, legacy Twin1 framing, or an earlier project formulation and must not override V2. PROCEDURAL messages contain task handoff, status or conversational routing rather than additional technical requirements.
No category authorizes deletion of a still-valid technical requirement. If a future audit finds a requirement in an INCORPORATED message that is absent from the cited destination chapters, reclassify that item RESTORE REQUIRED, add it to the relevant chapter with a source citation, and rerun all source, contract and render checks.
Classification | Recovered message IDs | Treatment in this specification
DIRECT | 02b66d75, 0d82fc95, 1587848f, 1df20739, 2cb65fd3, 351639f6, 47895faf, 4a730f2d, 54c66632, 555cd78f, 5bc1ca6f, 5f8d2c40, 71644636, 752eb594, 81c5e230, 8a2f4b20, ba0228df, bd6cf7ce, c8e21db3, c8fbc0e5, d11b5930, d6fc5b9c, ee248bf5, f0865d4e | Cited directly in Chapters 2-29.
INCORPORATED | 06e6c14a, 5dd8c6ba, d44bc6fa, 9ea8468e, 3219e264, bb044d0d, 80d177e9, 36bd2db0, b3e05c2c, 1be6895b, 7c220cef, 1efc5ba1, 15e3645d, 0782accb, 4c1ed767, 2ef93d15 | Dataset/prior-art, full integration, navigation, early domain-specialization, RBP, core-belief, twin-pair, research-principle and architecture-correction material is incorporated into Chapters 2-30. Where a procedural message stated a durable rule, that rule is represented in the frozen boundaries or fidelity ledger.
## Corpus gate
Before publishing a later revision, run a source-corpus gate:
1. Enumerate every recovered message ID.
2. Confirm each ID appears exactly once in this register.
3. Confirm every DIRECT message has a source citation in its destination chapter.
4. Re-read every INCORPORATED message against the final destination chapters.
5. For each conflict, apply the later explicit V2 decision; for each omission, restore and cite it.
6. Confirm no SUPERSEDED source reintroduces Twin1, Model2A/B/C naming, or a contradictory boundary.
7. Render and inspect the revised DOCX after any restoration.
# 32. Model Construction Readiness Audit
## Scope of this audit
This chapter answers a narrower and more important question than document completeness: can a software agent build each model package faithfully from this specification? The answer must distinguish a configurable experimental implementation from a canonical final neural architecture. A module is CANDIDATE-READY when its contracts, state, inputs, outputs, update/training path, evaluation and failure boundaries are sufficient for an implementation behind a configuration. It is FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY only when the source selected the exact architecture, dimensions, training configuration and data authority required for a canonical implementation.
The recovered source explicitly leaves exact neural implementations open for Model 2 Core, 2T, 2E, 2S and Model 1. Therefore no agent may reinterpret a candidate-ready result as final-architecture-ready.
## Package-by-package result
Package | Inputs/state/output/lifecycle specified | Training, losses, baselines, tests specified | Candidate implementation status | Final architecture status | Exact unresolved decision
Model 2 Core | Yes. Chapters 2-3 define evidence, belief cell, uncertainty, provenance, batch tensors, association, BUO, RBP, TBD, PMBL and the predict/correct forward sequence. | Yes. Chapters 3-9 define staged curriculum, masked objectives, baselines, invariants and core experiments. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Exact encoder/fusion, association scorer, RBP message network, temporal family, uncertainty estimator, aggregation, dimensions and loss weights are OPEN/EXPERIMENTAL.

Modality training/loss sections and missing-modality checks are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Per-modality backbone, cross-modal fusion operator, embedding dimension and quality-head architecture.

Sanity, mechanism-shift, observation, calibration and Twin-usefulness experiments are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Target-material parameter priors, stochastic calibration and any learned residual.
Model 2T and TCDP | Yes. Structural graph, factorized belief, direct versus propagated confidence, mechanism-conditioned messages, heads and temporal boundary are specified. | Yes. T1-T8 training, losses, baselines B1-B8, 2T-E01-E10 and completion gates are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Exact 2T neural blocks, factor dimensions, message/update heads and qualified structural data manifest.
Twin2E and MEIFE | Yes. Habitat, environmental fields, entity state, interaction/dynamics, observation levels and truth layers are specified. | Yes. Field/entity/coupling validation, calibration and Twin comparisons are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Field solver/surrogate, stochastic entity dynamics and target-environment priors.
Model 2E and CEFD | Yes. Heterogeneous entity-plus-field state, bidirectional coupling, multi-scale aggregation, outputs and uncertainty boundaries are specified. | Yes. E1-E9 curriculum, entity/field/CEFD losses, E01-E12 and uncoupled/cross-attention/GNN baselines are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Field representation, entity encoder, heterogeneous update network, coupling operator, temporal backbone and vetted paired data.
Twin2S and OCPWE | Yes. Canonical geometry, visibility/occlusion truth, procedural world controls, counterfactuals and observation generator are specified. | Yes. Visibility, trajectory, randomization and transfer checks are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Target-domain assets, sensor priors and realism calibration.
Model 2S and UAHSM | Yes. Truth/observability/belief separation, observed/inferred/unknown status, hierarchical memory, direct update, relational update and adaptive resolution are specified. | Yes. S1-S10 curriculum, loss/status/unsupported-confidence objectives, 2S-E01-E16 and map baselines are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Spatial representation and learned update/retrieval backbone.
Model 1 and EGDC | Yes. Decision Claim Graph, belief query, grounded proposal, action vocabulary, deterministic constraints, router and outcome monitor are specified. | Yes. Curriculum, loss families, UIR, baseline families and decision experiments are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Reasoning engine, ranking/value model, aggregation and learning objective.
MCBR | Yes. InformationNeed, knowledge gap, candidate observation, observability, hypothesis discrimination, ObservationPlan and stop condition are specified. | Yes. Oracle supervision, curricula, regret/mission-value metrics and matched baselines are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Information-value estimator, hypothesis representation and learned versus analytical ranker.
BAAC | Yes. Receiver state, InformationUnit, semantic deltas, progressive fidelity, queue/reconciliation and channel contract are specified. | Yes. Oracle value, curriculum, communication baselines, bandwidth-collapse and outage experiments are specified. | CANDIDATE-READY. | NOT FINAL-ARCHITECTURE-READY. | Scheduler, learned value estimator, codec/fidelity predictor and channel predictor.
## Verdict
A software agent can build the entire V2 family as experimental, contract-faithful candidate implementations from this document. It can build the Twins, schemas, core, domain children, Model 1, MCBR, BAAC, baselines, training/evaluation harnesses and integrations without inventing a new subsystem.
A software agent cannot faithfully build the final proprietary neural architectures from this document alone. That is not an omission this audit can repair honestly, because the recovered design sources explicitly leave those architectures unresolved. The exact choices in the final column require experiments and ADRs under Chapter 30, or a separate source containing explicit final architecture decisions. Until then, every learned implementation must be marked EXPERIMENTAL_CANDIDATE with its complete configuration, baseline and evidence record.
## Mandatory implementation handoff
Before any coding agent starts a learned package, it must create docs/IMPLEMENTATION_MAP.md with one row per package above:
package_id
source_chapters
input_schema
output_schema
truth_access_policy
implementation_status
candidate_config_path
baseline_config_paths
losses_and_masks
train_val_test_ood_manifest
unit_and_leakage_tests
experiment_ids
open_decisions
ADR_ids
The agent must stop and report OPEN_BLOCKED when it reaches a final-architecture decision not selected by source or ADR. It may not substitute a familiar Transformer, GNN, GRU, generic agent, LLM, NeRF, database, scheduler or simulator setting and call it Conrad V2.
# 33. Proposed Canonical Neural Implementation Freeze V0 2
## Status and authority
PROPOSED IMPLEMENTATION FREEZE. This chapter supplies the layer-level implementation that the recovered source deliberately did not select. It does not alter the frozen Conrad V2 subsystem boundaries: Twins remain truth worlds, Model 2 remains the belief world, and Model 1 remains the decision world. It is a coherent V0.2 candidate for the whole family, not a claim that the historical source had already selected these layers.
After Kerem implements this chapter, every run must use architecture_id: conrad_v2_impl_freeze_v0_2 and retain all ablations required by Chapters 3-27. A later ADR may replace a component only when the replacement beats this configuration under the registered comparator protocol.
## Shared dimensions and primitives
All learned modules use PyTorch float32 for training, autocast bfloat16 where supported, and float32 accumulation for losses and uncertainty calculations.
architecture_id: conrad_v2_impl_freeze_v0_2
seed_set: [2026201, 2026202, 2026203]
De: 256                 # encoded evidence token
Dz: 256                 # belief latent
Du: 64                  # four-channel uncertainty latent
Dt: 64                  # temporal context
Dh: 128                 # compressed history
Dr: 64                  # relation embedding
Dm: 128                 # mechanism embedding
heads: 8
head_dim: 32
dropout: 0.10
norm: layernorm
activation: gelu
optimizer: adamw
weight_decay: 0.01
grad_clip_norm: 1.0
Every residual block uses LayerNorm -> Linear -> GELU -> Dropout -> Linear -> Dropout -> residual add. Every attention block is pre-norm multi-head attention with 8 heads of 32 dimensions, followed by that residual feed-forward block with hidden width 1024. All masking is explicit: padded evidence, unavailable modalities, unavailable labels, absent graph nodes and invalid candidate actions must contribute zero attention weight and zero loss denominator contribution.
class ResidualMLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_in: int, d_hidden: int, d_out: int, dropout: float = 0.10): ...

class PreNormTransformerBlock(nn.Module):
    # d_model=256, nhead=8, ffn=1024, dropout=0.10
    def forward(self, tokens, key_padding_mask=None, attn_bias=None): ...
## ECMER exact implementation
Each observation produces one or more EvidenceToken[De=256]. The modality encoder never emits a belief; it emits evidence plus quality and provenance metadata.
Modality | Exact encoder | Output
RGB or monocular image | Conrad-owned ResNet-18 initialized randomly through layer4; global average pool -> 512 -> 256 projection -> LayerNorm. Pretrain all layers with the in-house self-supervised program below, then fine-tune end-to-end. No public pretrained checkpoint may initialize the canonical model. | One image token plus 16 patch-summary tokens from adaptive 4x4 pooling, each 512 -> 256.
Forward/side-scan sonar image | Separate Conrad-owned ResNet-18 initialized randomly; identical 512 -> 256 projection. Pretrain with the same in-house program, with no weight sharing with RGB. | One global plus 16 patch-summary tokens.
Point/range/depth cloud | Voxelize to a 32 x 32 x 32 local grid; three Conv3d(32,64,128) stages with kernel 3, stride 2, GroupNorm and GELU; global pool -> 128 -> 256. | One geometry token.
Scalar vector sensors | Standardize per sensor using train split only; M -> 128 -> 256 -> 256 ResidualMLP with a missingness bit per scalar. | One sensor token.
Pose/time/context | position(3), quaternion(4), covariance summary(6), delta_t, sensor_id, frame_id -> 16 -> 128 -> 256 MLP plus learned sensor/frame embeddings. | Added to every modality token.
For one observation event, concatenate all valid modality tokens, prepend a learned [EVENT] token, add learned modality/type embeddings and pass through four PreNormTransformerBlocks. The [EVENT] output is the event embedding e_k in R^256.
event_tokens = concat(valid_modality_tokens) + pose_time_context
event_tokens = prepend(event_token, event_tokens)
event_tokens = transformer4(event_tokens, key_padding_mask=mask)
e_k = event_tokens[:, 0]
quality = quality_head(concat(e_k, modality_availability, raw_quality_features))
# quality_head:  (256 + Q) -> 256 -> 128 -> [reliability, log_var_A, ood_logit, usable_logit]
quality_head uses sigmoid for reliability and usability, a clipped [-10, 5] log variance for aleatoric uncertainty, and a binary OOD logit. ECMER losses are the source-defined available modality/task losses plus: contrastive same-entity/different-view InfoNCE, masked-modality reconstruction from [EVENT], reliability Brier loss, and heteroscedastic NLL. No image-only target may supervise an unavailable structural or ecological head.
## Conrad-owned self-supervised representation pretraining
The canonical RGB and sonar encoder weights are created from random initialization and trained by Conrad before any task-specific fine-tuning. The pretraining corpus may contain only: (1) Twin2S/Twin2T/Twin2E and Unity observations generated under versioned scenarios; and (2) rights-cleared raw observations captured by Conrad or supplied to Conrad with recorded permission. Public datasets may be used only through their approved manifests and never as a source of imported model weights.
Pretraining runs before ECMER task training and records representation_pretraining_id, scenario/twin versions, raw-data manifest IDs, split hash, random seed and augmentation configuration in every encoder checkpoint. A checkpoint lacking those fields cannot initialize the canonical RGB or sonar encoder.
For each modality, use a projection head 256 -> 1024 -> 256 during pretraining and discard it before ECMER fine-tuning. The required objective is the weighted sum of: augmented-view contrastive alignment; temporal positive-pair alignment for the same truth entity/region across nearby observations; cross-modal correspondence alignment where the twin or calibrated real capture supplies a valid pairing; and masked-patch/token reconstruction. Positives never cross a scenario or lineage split. Hard negatives include visually similar but different entities, regions and degradation states. The projection head is not part of deployed inference.
L_repr = L_view_contrast + L_temporal + L_cross_modal + L_masked_reconstruction
Use AdamW at 3e-4 for the randomly initialized RGB and sonar encoders and projection heads during representation pretraining, cosine decay with 5% warm-up, the existing effective batch size 64, and the same deterministic seed/replay rules. Downstream ECMER fine-tuning uses 1e-4 for these pretrained in-house encoders and 3e-4 for newly initialized heads and fusion layers.
BASELINE-ECMER-IMGNET is permitted only as a noncanonical comparator: the same ResNet-18 topology initialized from ImageNet, evaluated with the identical data manifests, budget, augmentations, task losses and held-out splits. It must be stored under baseline_family: external_initialization; it may not be promoted, exported as a Conrad canonical checkpoint, or silently substituted for the random-initialized Conrad encoder. BASELINE-ECMER-RANDOM-SUPERVISED trains the same encoder without self-supervised pretraining.
The required selection report compares CONRAD-SSL, BASELINE-ECMER-IMGNET and BASELINE-ECMER-RANDOM-SUPERVISED on representation correspondence, downstream evidence quality, calibration, in-domain/OOD robustness, sample efficiency and compute cost. The V0.2 canonical path remains Conrad self-supervised pretraining unless an ADR, based on this matched report, replaces it. This experiment measures practical performance; it does not change the ownership of the Conrad architecture.
## Association exact implementation
For each evidence token, retrieve at most K=32 belief candidates using a spatial-temporal index. Build pair features:
[e_k(256), z_i(256), e_k-z_i(256), e_k*z_i(256),
  relative_pose(12), time_features(4), edge/context(Dr=64),
  reliability(1), uncertainty_summary(4)] = 853 dimensions
Project 853 -> 512 -> 256 -> 128 -> 1 with GELU, LayerNorm after the first two hidden layers and dropout 0.10. Append a learned NO_MATCH candidate with a separate 256 -> 128 -> 1 scorer. Softmax is over valid retrieved candidates plus NO_MATCH. During training use cross-entropy for known association, focal loss on rare no-match cases, and a pairwise ranking term that requires the correct identity to exceed every valid negative by margin 0.20. At inference, accept an association only if its probability is at least 0.60 and exceeds the runner-up by 0.15; otherwise create a tentative belief or emit NO_MATCH according to PMBL policy.
## BUO exact implementation
For each associated pair (belief i, evidence k), compute:
x = concat(z_i, e_k, z_i - e_k, z_i * e_k, u_i, quality_k, temporal_i) # 256+256+256+256+64+4+64
h = residual_mlp_1156_512_256(x)
trust = sigmoid(mlp_256_128_1(h))
innovation = mlp_256_512_256(h)
gate = sigmoid(mlp_256_128_256(h))
z_direct = layer_norm(z_i + gate * innovation)
u_direct = uncertainty_update(concat(u_i, h, quality_k))
uncertainty_update is 324 -> 256 -> 128 -> 64; it predicts deltas to four 16-dimensional partitions U_A, U_E, U_C, U_O. The exposed scalar channels are softplus projections of their respective partition. Duplicate evidence is rejected before this block. Multiple direct observations at the same time are set-aggregated by two attention pooling blocks, then one BUO update is committed with every consumed evidence ID recorded. The direct update loss is: available masked state/head loss + latent consistency loss only where synthetic truth permits it + calibrated NLL + contradiction ranking + innovation L2 penalty 1e-4.
## RBP exact implementation
RBP uses three relation-conditioned graph-transformer layers. For directed edge j -> i, define a relation embedding r_ji from typed relation ID, geometry, distance, age and edge-confidence using R^* -> 128 -> 64 MLP. Each layer uses eight heads:
q_i = Wq[z_i, u_i]
k_ji = Wk[z_j, r_ji, provenance_strength]
v_ji = Wv[z_j, r_ji, source_uncertainty]
alpha_ji = masked_softmax((q_i @ k_ji) / sqrt(32) + relation_bias(r_ji))
m_i = concat_heads(sum_j(alpha_ji * v_ji))
delta_i = gate_i * Wo(m_i)
z_i = layer_norm(z_i + delta_i)
gate_i = sigmoid(MLP([z_i, m_i, u_i], 576 -> 256 -> 256)). Each relational revision is labelled INFERRED, contains source belief/edge revisions, and cannot lower observational uncertainty as if it were direct sensing. RBP losses are downstream masked state loss, edge consistency, uncertainty calibration, and a contamination penalty on misleading-edge episodes.
## TBD and PMBL exact implementation
PMBL is deterministic lifecycle logic around the learned state. Working memory stores latest GPU tensors; persistent storage stores every committed revision, evidence references, graph lineage and compact history; archive is immutable raw evidence plus retired lineage. Learned retrieval is not enabled in V0.1.
TBD uses domain-specific transition modules behind one interface:
class TBD(nn.Module):
    def forward(self, z, u, temporal, delta_t_s, context) -> tuple[z_pred, u_pred, temporal_pred]: ...
The shared temporal stem is concat(z(256), u(64), temporal(64), log1p(delta_t), context(64)) -> 512 -> 256, followed by a GRUCell(input=256, hidden=256). The GRU hidden is projected to z_pred, while 256 -> 128 -> 64 predicts uncertainty drift. Use unrolled sequence length 32, truncated BPTT at 32, and detach only across persisted episode boundaries. The domain residual/context blocks below specialize the transition without changing the external contract.
## Model 2T and Twin2T exact implementation
Twin2T is primarily deterministic/mechanistic. Each component has corrosion/material-loss and fatigue/crack state, mechanism parameters sampled from a logged scenario prior, and stochastic residual epsilon ~ N(0, sigma_mechanism). V0.1 has no learned Twin2T residual. A simulation tick performs corrosion update, fatigue update, coupling update, event/intervention update, then observation masking. This preserves inspectable truth and makes MCDE parameters directly auditable.
Model 2T uses z_T = [z_material(64), z_degradation(64), z_load(48), z_geometry(48), z_history(32)]. State heads are:
condition:          256 -> 128 -> C_condition
degradation_type:   256 -> 128 -> C_degradation
severity:           256 -> 128 -> 1
corrosion_depth:    256 -> 128 -> 1
crack_length:       256 -> 128 -> 1
change_state:       256 -> 128 -> C_change
forecast_mean/logv: 256 -> 128 -> 2 per target
TCDP uses three mechanism-conditioned message layers. For an edge, mechanism embedding is a learned 128-vector selected from corrosion, fatigue, crack, load-path, repair/intervention and generic relation types. Message MLP is concat(z_j(256), z_i(256), r_ji(64), mechanism(128), delta_t(1)) -> 512 -> 256 -> 256; message gate is 512 -> 128 -> 1. Aggregate by reliability-weighted attention, then apply the RBP update. The 2T TBD context block is concat(shared_gru_output, mechanism_summary(128), engineering_delta(256)) -> 512 -> 256, where engineering_delta comes from the deterministic MCDE prior. Training stages, masks, losses and B1-B8 baselines remain mandatory.
## Model 2E and Twin2E exact implementation
Twin2E is a logged mechanistic simulator: habitat geometry, fields, entities, interactions, disturbances and recovery. Environmental fields are fixed-resolution tensors at V0.1: regional 64 x 64 x 8, local 32 x 32 x 16; entity dynamics are agent/state rules. Field transition uses finite-difference advection-diffusion with scenario-sampled coefficients; entity transition uses rate-limited stochastic state rules. No learned Twin2E component is permitted before calibration data exists.
Model 2E has separate entity and field streams. Entity tokens use entity_features -> 128 -> 256, then three heterogeneous graph-transformer layers with 8 heads and relation embeddings of 64. Field tensors pass through a 3-level 3D U-Net: channels C -> 64 -> 128 -> 256, each level has two Conv3d(kernel=3,padding=1) + GroupNorm(8) + GELU blocks; decoder 256 -> 128 -> 64 -> C_field_out with skip connections. CEFD coupling happens twice per time step:
field_to_entity = cross_attention(query=entity_tokens, key_value=sampled_field_tokens)
entity_to_field = splat_entity_messages(entity_tokens, positions, grid_shape)
entity_tokens = entity_blocks(entity_tokens + field_to_entity)
field_state = field_unet(field_state + entity_to_field)
Cross-attention has 8 heads, 256 model dimension and distance/scale bias. The coupling gate is sigmoid(MLP([entity(256), sampled_field(256), uncertainty(64)], 576 -> 256 -> 1)). Entity losses, field NLL/reconstruction/gradient losses, CEFD counterfactual coupling loss and uncertainty terms remain exactly as Chapters 12-13 require.
## Model 2S and Twin2S exact implementation
Twin2S uses deterministic procedural geometry and a visibility oracle. Canonical truth uses signed-distance/occupancy on a sparse voxel-octree with base voxel 0.25 m, refinement levels 0.125 m and 0.0625 m. These are simulation configuration defaults, not physical-map claims. OCPWE stores independent world truth, observability truth and trajectory/observation truth. No Twin2S geometry tensor or hidden ID enters Model 2S inference.
UAHSM stores sparse cells. Each active cell state is z_S(256), u(64), status_logits(4), age, observation_count. Local observations encode through ECMER and update cells via concat(z_cell, e_local, relative_pose, pose_covariance, coverage) -> 512 -> 256 -> 256 gated residual update. Neighbor propagation uses two local sparse graph-attention layers; adaptive refinement uses a deterministic policy based on coverage, uncertainty gradient and geometry discontinuity, not a learned policy in V0.1. Global and regional context use two Transformer blocks over at most 256 selected region tokens. Spatial heads are occupancy/SDF mean and log variance, semantic class, observed/inferred/predicted/unknown status and change score. The status head is trained with cross-entropy and the unsupported-confidence penalty from Chapter 14.
## Model 1 EGDC exact implementation
EGDC is not an LLM wrapper. It consumes a bounded Decision Claim Graph with at most 128 claim nodes, each claim_embedding(256) + uncertainty(64) + provenance summary(32) + age/context(32) -> 384 -> 256. Encode with four graph-transformer layers, 8 heads and typed claim/evidence/contradiction edges. Generate actions only from the frozen constrained vocabulary. Each valid action is embedded to 128 and scored against a [MISSION] graph token:
action_score = MLP([mission_token(256), action_embedding(128), resource_state(32), risk_features(32)], 448 -> 256 -> 128 -> 1)
claim_support = attention(action_query, relevant_claim_nodes)
The action score is used only to rank proposals. A deterministic constraint engine validates all action arguments, freshness, health, power, risk and motion constraints. EGDC loss is cross-entropy/ranking on oracle or adjudicated actions, support-edge supervision, outcome prediction loss and UIR penalty. The constraint engine is never optimized away.
## MCBR exact implementation
Generate up to 128 feasible observation candidates from geometry, sensor ranges and navigation constraints. Each candidate vector includes target belief 256, uncertainty 64, hypothesis embedding 128, viewpoint/sensor/configuration 64, predicted observability features 64, navigation cost/risk/energy 32 and mission context 64. Project to 256, cross-attend over relevant belief/claim tokens with three Transformer blocks, then heads:
visibility probability: 256 -> 128 -> 1 sigmoid
oracle-value prediction: 256 -> 128 -> 1
hypothesis discrimination: 256 -> 128 -> 1
cost/risk correction: 256 -> 128 -> 2
candidate score: predicted mission value - constrained costs
Train value regression against Twin-executed oracle belief-error reduction; train ranking with pairwise logistic loss and calibrate visibility with Brier loss. Feasibility and safety constraints filter before ranking. MCBR outputs an ObservationPlan, never a thruster command.
## BAAC exact implementation
Receiver state is a set of up to 256 belief-summary tokens. Sender InformationUnits and receiver tokens are encoded by separate features -> 256 MLPs, then pass through two cross-attention blocks. Per unit heads predict receiver novelty, expected mission value, expected information loss at fidelity levels F0-F4, bits, latency and deadline violation probability. The scheduler is deterministic constrained greedy for V0.1:
priority = (mission_value * novelty * confidence_adjustment) / max(predicted_bits, 1)
select units in descending priority while respecting bandwidth, deadline, energy and link constraints
The learned heads estimate values; they do not directly bypass the queue constraints. Semantic delta encoding uses revision IDs and changed fields; raw evidence remains retrievable. Train on channel-simulation oracle values with Huber value loss, BCE novelty/deadline losses and fidelity reconstruction/utility loss.
## Training configuration and implementation gates
Use AdamW with learning rate 3e-4 for new MLP/GNN/Transformer parameters and 1e-4 for in-house self-supervised pretrained image backbones during downstream training, cosine decay with 5% linear warm-up, batch size 16 sequence episodes per GPU, gradient accumulation to effective batch 64, maximum 200 epochs, early stopping after 25 validation epochs without primary-metric improvement. Choose checkpoints only on validation metrics. Test and OOD are final-only.
Every package must ship: immutable YAML config, schema fixture, input/output shape test, mask/permutation test, truth-leakage test, deterministic replay test, baseline config, ablation config, checkpoint metadata and experiment record. A package fails implementation review if any tensor, modality, relation, loss term, label availability mask, uncertainty channel, provenance path or open decision is silently omitted.
The first integrated V0.1 run is 2S plus 2T: OCPWE/MCDE truth -> Unity observations -> ECMER -> association/BUO/RBP/TBD/PMBL -> UAHSM/TCDP -> MCBR -> navigation -> evidence update -> EGDC decision -> BAAC message. 2E runs in parallel with the same interfaces and does not block this minimum serious demonstration.
# 34. Proposed Software Stack Closure Freeze V0.2
## Authority and purpose
PROPOSED IMPLEMENTATION FREEZE. Chapter 33 selected the first codeable neural architectures. This chapter selects the minimal operational substrate needed to create, test, run, reproduce and recover that architecture from an empty repository. It adds no intelligence subsystem and does not alter the frozen separation: Twins are truth worlds, Model 2 is the belief world and Model 1 is the decision world.
architecture_id: conrad_v2_impl_freeze_v0_2 and stack_id: conrad_v2_stack_freeze_v0_2 must be recorded on every run, checkpoint, replay and physical log. A later ADR can replace a choice here only after a compatible migration and matched evidence.
## Supported platform and dependency authority
The reference development and robot runtime target is Ubuntu 22.04 or later on x86_64. Python is exactly 3.11.x. GPU execution uses a CUDA build compatible with the installed NVIDIA driver; CPU execution must support all schemas, twins, tests, replay and baseline models. The hardware process may run without a GPU, but no process may silently fall back from an intended GPU training run without recording device_fallback: true.
Use one Python monorepo managed by uv. pyproject.toml is the only direct dependency declaration and uv.lock is committed. A clean checkout is valid only when the following commands succeed:
uv sync --all-groups
uv run ruff check .
uv run mypy conrad tests
uv run pytest -q
uv run conrad doctor
Production dependency groups are fixed by responsibility, not by unpinned convenience imports:
[project]
requires-python = ">=3.11,<3.12"
dependencies = [
  "pydantic>=2,<3", "numpy>=1.26,<3", "scipy>=1.11,<2",
  "torch>=2.4,<2.7", "opencv-python>=4.9,<5", "pyyaml>=6,<7",
  "sqlalchemy>=2,<3", "alembic>=1.13,<2", "mlflow>=2,<4",
  "typer>=0.12,<1", "structlog>=24,<26", "pyzmq>=26,<28"
]
[dependency-groups]
dev = ["pytest>=8,<9", "pytest-cov>=5,<7", "hypothesis>=6,<7", "ruff>=0.6,<1", "mypy>=1.11,<2"]
Exact resolved versions live only in uv.lock; the ranges above are compatibility boundaries, not permission to overwrite a validated lock. Add Unity, ROS, camera-SDK or thruster-controller bindings only in isolated adapters. Core belief, model, twin and decision packages must import no vendor SDK.
## Required repository and ownership layout
The Chapter 30 closure map is expanded to this required physical layout. Every executable path has a unit-test mirror and every public import has a schema-level contract test.
conrad/
  __main__.py                    # typer CLI: doctor, sim, train, eval, replay, runtime
  settings.py                    # typed configuration loading and immutable run snapshot
  schemas/                       # Pydantic v2 public contracts only
  core/ domains/ twins/ decision/ active/ communication/ robotics/ sim/ data/ training/ evaluation/
  runtime/
    supervisor.py                # single-process lifecycle state machine
    event_log.py                 # append-only event envelopes
    health.py                    # availability/freshness/deadline state
    command_gateway.py           # final validated RobotHardwareInterface boundary
  persistence/
    db.py migrations/ repository.py object_store.py replay_store.py
  adapters/
    unity/ hardware/ datasets/ telemetry/
  cli/

  base/ sim/ train/ eval/ runtime/ robot/

  unit/ property/ contract/ integration/ replay/ hardware_stub/ acceptance/

  bootstrap.sh lint.sh test.sh train.sh eval.sh replay.sh runtime.sh
